The licensed max_abs is 0.25–8.00 bf16 ULPs of a row's own largest element, so one ULP at the top of a tensor is sometimes inside the band and sometimes outside it — the two relative metrics are what actually bind: the worst row used 2.581e-01 of the 1-cos licence and 5.010e-01 of the meanrel one, and 5.000e-01 of the max_abs one.

Ladder depth is the storage width's own mantissa count: past it the perturbation rounds away and is not a defect any band could see.
